DAX momentum · full research pipeline

2020–2023 development / 2024 onward retrospective out-of-sample

Research only. Fails one or more validation gates; do not call it a validated improvement. Live EAs, BATs, presets and website remain unchanged.

208 unique settings; 243 screening / neighbourhood passes. 2020–2022 searches, 2023 finalist check, frozen settings before all 2024+ evaluation. Raw PF 1.11 misses the usual raw gate: optimisation is exploratory.

All native runs start with $10,000, use a 1% equity-risk target, and include broker commissions, spread, swaps and 150 ms execution delay. Calendar-period runs restart separately; yearly returns must not be added. Requested endpoint is today; latest available OOS broker quote: 2026-10-06 23:59:52 UTC. Today is provisional and may be missing or incomplete. Final tester liquidations are included.

History limitations: no real ticks in development. Older OOS history is generated; real ticks begin in 2026. “Every tick based on real ticks” mode falls back where broker real ticks are missing. These are DAX CFD results, not futures/NQ results.

Cost warning: sampled 2026 entry quotes have identical bid and ask. A zero extra spread is not a stress test. Native commission/swap remain included, but the mandatory broker-extra-cost gate is unavailable / failed. The recent results may be optimistic if this quote feed does not reflect executable spreads.

The baseline’s later history was viewed in prior work, so this is a retrospective temporal holdout—not a completely untouched experiment. No parameter re-selection occurred after 2024+ evaluation.

DION, period 14
Additional filtersResearchADXMinimum = 15.00; Skip Monday and Friday
Initial stop0.60% of entry price
Profit target3.00R
Trailing stopATR14 × 4.00, arms at 1.00R
Break-even / dynamicCompleted M15 close reaches 50.00% of initial TP distance → lock 20.00% of TP distance; this is a one-level lock, alongside any ATR/MA trail
Time exitNo maximum hold
Sizing1% current equity target; original upward lot-step / broker-minimum rounding retained. Not a guaranteed loss cap.
ScopeTester-only research binary; cannot attach to a live chart. No live source, preset, BAT or website change.

PF / wins are calculated from net position P&L including all deal costs. DD is native maximum relative floating-equity DD, not closed-balance DD. Sharpe uses daily equity returns including zero-return calendar days (365 annualisation), not chart-sensitive MT5 Sharpe.
